all colours muted, earthy and weathered, nothing bright, among them the same recurring faces in every scene, always in the same clothes: Jory (about 18, lanky, messy blond hair, grey wool cap, faded woad-blue tunic, sheepskin vest), Ulf (big man in his 30s, shaved head, sheepskin vest over a rust-brown tunic, iron gaff), Sef (wiry man in his 30s, black beard, crooked nose, dark oiled-leather jerkin over a mustard-ochre tunic), Garth (about 60, bald, short grey beard, grey oilskin cape over an oatmeal tunic), Brenna (fishwife in her 40s, red-brown braid, walnut-brown kirtle, cream wool shawl) and Mags (about 70, white hair, sheep-grey kirtle, black wool shawl); everyone else keeps their hood up, faces in shadow; people of different heights and builds; Bram, a burly man about 40 with a scarred jaw, wet curly red-brown hair and a thick matted red-brown beard, wearing a heavy, shaggy, knee-length coat of worn brown and tan sheepskin fleece with a wide fleece collar, over a stained brown wool tunic with a wide leather belt, rough brown wool trousers and tall worn brown leather boots; Maren, a woman about 30 with long wet tangled dark-brown hair with thin braids, pale weathered freckled skin, tired grey-green eyes, wearing her winter clothes: a long, heavy black wool hooded cloak reaching her ankles, with a ragged, frayed hem, the deep hood up over her head, fastened at the throat with a round dark iron disc clasp; under it a long black wool dress with a long brown leather belt whose end hangs down the front; thick dark grey knitted wool mittens; worn dark leather ankle boots; Tal, a fully grown adult sea dragon only about 6 feet (1.8 m) tall at the head, the size of a horse, not giant and not towering: standing on all four legs his head is level with a grown woman's head, and his body is about as long as a horse, just big enough to carry one woman on his back; slender and strong, with blush-pink and rose-white scales with a soft pearly sheen, large pale pink bat-like wings with faint violet veins, a crest of sharp spiky pink-white fin-frills on his head and neck, and violet slit-pupil eyes. Mood: explosive. Faint breath vapor in the cold air. Nobody looks into the camera lens. All mouths closed. Palette: storm slate grey, fog grey, driftwood brown and wet-wool grey, with orange mob pitch torches against cold blue. No text, no watermark, no modern objects. Setting: the rocky shore at the mouth of the hidden sea cave at night: a narrow strip of wet black shingle and kelp-covered rocks running straight down into the open sea; the dark sea is right there, low waves washing onto the pebbles just a few meters behind Tal, white foam in the torchlight, the cave mouth a black shape in the cliff on the far left.
